<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>越會</rb><rt>yüih-we</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>展開來</rb><rt>tsin-kʽæ-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>重頭</rb><rt>djong-deo</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>辛辛</rb><rt>sing-sing</rt><rb>苦苦</rb><rt>kwʽu-kwʽu</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>舂</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>石灰</rb><rt>zah-hwe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>燒</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>工程</rb><rt>kong-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>用工個</rb><rt>yüong-kong-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>管勒</rb><rt>kwun-leh</rt><rb>嚴</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>收生</rb><rt>siu-sang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>實拉</rb><rt>Jih-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>部亞</rb><rt>Bu-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>收生</rb><rt>siu-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>坐桶</rb><rt>zo-dong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>娃</rb><rt>w̆æn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>收生個</rb><rt>siu-sang-go</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>男娃</rb><rt>nen-w̆æn</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>收生個</rb><rt>siu-sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>男娃</rb><rt>nen-w̆æn</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>收生個</rb><rt>Siu-sang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>koh-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>健</rb><rt>gyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>收生個</rb><rt>siu-sang-go</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生落了</rb><rt>sang-loh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>收生個</rb><rt>siu-sang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>強盛</rb><rt>gyiang-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>收生個</rb><rt>siu-sang-go</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>成家立業</rb><rt>dzing-kô-lih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>男娃</rb><rt>nen-w̆æn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>丟來</rb><rt>tiu-læ</rt><rb>河裏</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

### C. II.

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>KA</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囥了</rb><rt>kʽông-leh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>囥弗住</rb><rt>kʽông-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>蘆竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>箱子</rb><rt>siang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石油</rb><rt>zah-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乳漿</rb><rt>jü-tsiang</rt><rb>塗勒</rb><rt>du-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>河邊沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>蘆柴縫裏</rb><rt>lu-za-vong-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>河邊沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰肉</rb><rt>gyiang-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河邊沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箱子</rb><rt>siang-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蘆柴縫裏</rb><rt>lu-za-vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>開開來</rb><rt>Kʽæ-kʽæ-læ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>叫</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿哺</rb><rt>ah-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>奶花個</rb><rt>na-hwun-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>抱</rb><rt>Bao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囡個</rb><rt>nön-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>水裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上來個</rb><rt>zông-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Te-cʽih. 就是𠡒出。（原註）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壯年</rb><rt>công-nyin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>挑擔</rb><rt>tʽiao-tæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>Kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>沙泥裏</rb><rt>sô-nyi-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相打</rb><rt>siang-tang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗成禮</rb><rt>feh-dzing-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>破了</rb><rt>pʽo-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水槽</rb><rt>shü-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末子</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-loh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫餅</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ping</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革順</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Cʽih-meng nying. 就是出門人。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘆氣</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>謳響</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>勞苦個</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘆氣個</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. III.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>葉羅</u>個</rb><rt>Yih-lo-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>外邊</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何烈</rb><rt>ʽO-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>刺棚樹</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-jü</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>刺棚樹</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-jü</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>刺棚裏</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>腳裏個</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立的個</rb><rt>lih-tih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遮勒</rb><rt>tsô-leh</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>督工</rb><rt>toh-kong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>謳響</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-loh-læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>寬闊</rb><rt>kwʽun-kwʽeh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>厚實</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶奶</rb><rt>na-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖個</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份個</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>自然而然</rb><rt>Z-JÜN-R-JÜN</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>YIU-GO</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>Z-JÜN</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>YIU-GO</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代個</rb><rt>dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>話過了</rb><rt>wô-ko-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份個</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶奶</rb><rt>na-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖個</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>三日個</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手段</rb><rt>siu-dön</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>個打個</rb><rt>Ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>女客</rb><rt>nyü-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>銀子個</rb><rt>nying-ts-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Z-jün-r-jün yiu-go. 就是自然而然有個。（原註）</sup>

### C. IV.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>Kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>Tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>叉進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>叉進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>叉進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>叉進</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>河裏個</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒來</rb><rt>tao-læ</rt><rb>燥地裏</rb><rt>sao-di-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>河裏</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>馱來個</rb><rt>do-læ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>燥地裏</rb><rt>sao-di-li</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>口能</rb><rt>kʽeo-neng</rt><rb>舌辯</rb><rt>zih-bin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘴巴個</rb><rt>cü-pô-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啞子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聾𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮眼</rb><rt>liang-ngæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎眼個</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差個</rb><rt>tsʽa-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>託付</rb><rt>tʽoh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葉羅</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>轉去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>葉羅</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>穩穩</rb><rt>w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>性命個</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>騎</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手下個</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>長子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>長子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>下處裏</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>火石</rb><rt>ho-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>割</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>下身個</rb><rt>ʽô-sing-go</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出血個</rb><rt>cʽih-hyüih-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出血個</rb><rt>cʽih-hyüih-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>傳向</rb><rt>djün-hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>謳帶倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. V.

<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEO-DEO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>裏</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日個</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>刀槍</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歇擔</rb><rt>hyih-tæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>當日</rb><rt>Tông-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>督工個</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>磚頭個</rb><rt>cün-deo-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做滿</rb><rt>tso-mun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>懶惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>空頭</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>督工個</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拔</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>根株</rb><rt>keng-cü</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>督工個</rb><rt>Toh-kong-go</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>逐日</rb><rt>djoh-nyih</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>督工個</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>昨日</rb><rt>Zô-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>照數</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麥稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>過失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>懶惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>懶惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾<u>拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>麥</u>稈</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照數</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>Me-nyih</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>受害</rb><rt>ziu-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>Li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>路街裏</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城子個</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>賊臭個</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djoh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」
