### C. X.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>臣子個</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所做個</rb><rt>sô-tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>顯出個</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>低聲</rb><rt>ti-sing</rt><rb>小意</rb><rt>siao-i</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看弗見</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>壞掉個</rb><rt>wæ-diao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子個</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出世</rb><rt>cʽih-si</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>看見過個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt></ruby>。』」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>滅掉了</rb><rt>mih-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>少少</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前了</rb><rt>min-zin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>Sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>剩落東個</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>東風</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>東風</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>Wông-djong</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>停來</rb><rt>ding-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇種介個</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>Wông-djong</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地面頂</rb><rt>di-min-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>黑暗個</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>剩落東</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>樹裏個</rb><rt>jü-li-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樹裏</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt><rb>青青個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>召攏</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇遭個</rb><rt>keh-tsao-go</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>西風</rb><rt>si-fong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>吹進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只好</rb><rt>tsih-hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>摸來摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>墨漆地暗</rb><rt>moh-tsʽeh-di-en</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看弗見</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉處個</rb><rt>deng-cʽü-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>福禮</rb><rt>foh-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>蹄子</rb><rt>di-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>剩落東</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>見面個</rb><rt>kyin-min-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Keh heh-en ʽeo-ʽeo jih-jih, tsing-ziang moh-leh-djoh go. 或話，箇黑暗厚厚實實，正像摸勒着個。（原註）</sup>

---

### C. XI.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>金銀個</rb><rt>kying-nying-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>貴重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>半夜過</rb><rt>Pun-yia-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>牽磨個</rb><rt>kyʽin-mo-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>koh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>號淚大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇種介個</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>狗</rb><rt>keo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>伏帶倒</rb><rt>boh-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怏</rb><rt>do-ông</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>年終</rb><rt>nyin-cong</rt><rb>月份個</rb><rt>yüih-veng-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一束</rb><rt>ih-shoh</rt><rb>牛膝草</rb><rt>ngeo-sih-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浸勒</rb><rt>tsing-leh</rt><rb>碗裏個</rb><rt>un-li-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>碗裏個</rb><rt>un-li-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>搽來</rb><rt>djô-læ</rt><rb>門個</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>楣</rb><rt>mi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兩邊個</rb><rt>liang-pin-go</rt><rb>敦枋裏</rb><rt>teng-fông-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>逾越節個</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耽擱東個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-tong-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>外國</rb><rt>Nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雇來個</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一間</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拗斷</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>合公會</rb><rt>ʽeh-kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>koh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>本地</rb><rt>Peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XIII.

YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ feng-fu Mo-si, wô, <sup>2</sup> Yi-seh-lih ts-seng cong-nyiang, feh-leng nying teng sang-kʽeo, væn-pah deo-ih-tʽæ sang-cʽih-go, tu iao tseng-kyʽi peh Ngô: keh z joh-ü Ngô-go.


耶和華吩咐<u>摩西</u>，話：<sup>2</sup> 「<u>以色列</u>子孫中央，弗論人等牲口，凡百頭一胎生出個，都要尊起撥我。箇是屬於我個。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>走出個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水個</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞筆</rb><rt>Üô-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>月裏</rb><rt>yüih-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖個</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>禮節</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嘸酵水個</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嘸酵水個</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水個</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記認</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>額角</rb><rt>ngah-koh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>每年</rb><rt>me-nyin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>拗斷</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>頭一胎個</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>硬心</rb><rt>ngang-sing</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>頭一胎個</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>頭一胎個</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>長子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>額角</rb><rt>ngah-koh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>Kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>打仗個</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>走歸</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>轉彎</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>擺勒</rb><rt>pa-leh</rt><rb>陣勢</rb><rt>dzing-shü</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照顧</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>隔壁</rb><rt>kah-pih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雲個</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火個</rb><rt>ho-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>雲柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隱落</rb><rt>ing-loh</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>彎轉</rb><rt>w̆æn-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希錄</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>密奪</u>個</rb><rt>Mih-deh-go</rt><rb>中間</rb><rt>cong-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力洗分</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>迷路</rb><rt>mi-lu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>圍困了</rb><rt>we-kwʽeng-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>追趕</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>折轉</rb><rt>coh-cün</rt><rb>攻擊</rb><rt>kong-kyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>六百</rb><rt>loh-pah</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>帶領官</rb><rt>ta-ling-kwun</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>追趕</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>散腳</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah</rt><rb>散手</rb><rt>sæn-siu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>步兵</rb><rt>bu-ping</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>追趕</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希錄</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴力洗分</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng-go</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕着</rb><rt>ken-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近攏來了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>墳地</rb><rt>veng-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>裏</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>豈弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>吵擾</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>裏</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靜靜</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Sæn go nying. 或話，三個人。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>Eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥路</rb><rt>sao-lu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>追上來</rb><rt>tse-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>騎馬個</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趲過去</rb><rt>tsæn-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雲柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>趲過去</rb><rt>tsæn-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>營寨個</rb><rt>ying-za-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>近弗攏</rb><rt>gying-feh-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>東風</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>拉住了</rb><rt>lah-djü-leh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥路</rb><rt>sao-lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>左右</rb><rt>tso-yiu</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>牆</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>敦起了</rb><rt>teng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>趕上去</rb><rt>ken-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>快亮</rb><rt>kwʽa-liang</rt><rb>箇更</rb><rt>keh-kang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雲柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>散亂</rb><rt>sæn-lön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>行伍</rb><rt>ʽông-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>Tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>車子個</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts-go</rt><rb>輪盤</rb><rt>leng-bun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打仗了</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>合攏來</rb><rt>ʽeh-long-læ</rt><rb>浸沒</rb><rt>tsing-mah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>湧起</rb><rt>üong-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>流轉來</rb><rt>liu-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浸沒</rb><rt>tsing-mah</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕到</rb><rt>ken-tao</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>Tæn-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥路</rb><rt>sao-lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大牆</rb><rt>do-ziang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>左右</rb><rt>tso-yiu</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做撥</rb><rt>tso-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。
