### C. XV.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇首</rb><rt>keh-siu</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>騎馬個</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>力道</rb><rt>lih-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>唱個</rb><rt>tsʽông-go</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>頌揚</rb><rt>dzong-yiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊敬</rb><rt>tseng-kying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>勇將</rb><rt>üong-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兵隊</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>浸殺</rb><rt>tsing-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海裏</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>深深個</rb><rt>Sing-sing-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>沉</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>勇勇個</rb><rt>üong-üong-go</rt><rb>氣力</rb><rt>kyʽi-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>敲碎</rb><rt>kʽao-se</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>違逆</rb><rt>we-nyih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>威光</rb><rt>w̆e-kwông</rt><rb>敗</rb><rt>ba</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>枯落個</rb><rt>kwʽu-loh-go</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吹出</rb><rt>cʽü-cʽih</rt><rb>鼻頭管裏個</rb><rt>bih-deo-kwun-li-go</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>上落水</rb><rt>zông-loh-shü</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>高山</rb><rt>kao-sæn</rt><rb>堆</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>深深個</rb><rt>sing-sing-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>大水</rb><rt>do-shü</rt><rb>凝攏</rb><rt>nying-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>Dziu-dih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>追上</rb><rt>tse-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趕着</rb><rt>ken-djoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>擄掠來個</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>沉落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>洪水裏</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü-li</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>鉛</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>威嚴</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>氣力</rb><rt>kyʽi-lih</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>英勇</rb><rt>ing-üong</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>煬掉</rb><rt>yiang-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>Kying-hyiæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗聲</rb><rt>feh-sing</rt><rb>弗響</rb><rt>feh-hyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>山裏個</rb><rt>sæn-li-go</rt><rb>基業</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>備辦個</rb><rt>be-bæn-go</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>建造個</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>！<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Ting yiu yüong-wô. 或話，頂有榮華。（原註）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>搖頓鼓</rb><rt>yiao-teng-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>搖頓鼓</rb><rt>yiao-teng-kwu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>Ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>馬</rb><rt>Mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>騎馬個</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Ting yiu yüong-wô. 或話，頂有榮華。（原註）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>書珥</rb><rt>Shü-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>瑪拉</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-lah-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喫弗來</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>水裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>甜了</rb><rt>din-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>病個</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Kʽwu *ka-shih*. 就是「苦」解說。（原註）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>𡍲</rb><rt>tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棗子</rb><rt>tsao-ts</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>株</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。

### C. XVI.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>中間</rb><rt>cong-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>倒弗如</rb><rt>tao-feh-jü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>肉碗</rb><rt>nyüoh-un</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>餓煞</rb><rt>ngo-sah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>逐日</rb><rt>djoh-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>依從</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收進個</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>收進個</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一倍</rb><rt>ih-be</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>怨恨個</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng-go</rt><rb>說話了</rb><rt>shih-wô-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>怨恨個</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng-go</rt><rb>說話了</rb><rt>shih-wô-de</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會個</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>望過去</rb><rt>mông-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>怨恨個</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng-go</rt><rb>說話了</rb><rt>shih-wô-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鵪鶉</rb><rt>in-jing</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>飛攏來</rb><rt>fi-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>露水</rb><rt>lu-shü</rt><rb>鋪來</rb><rt>pʽu-læ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>鋪的個</rb><rt>Pʽu-tih-go</rt><rb>露水</rb><rt>lu-shü</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>小小個</rb><rt>siao-siao-go</rt><rb>圓個</rb><rt>yün-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>霜</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>細</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>看見仔</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人數</rb><rt>nying-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿嗎</rb><rt>ah-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>裏向個</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>人數</rb><rt>nying-su</rt><rb>收進去</rb><rt>siu-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收攏來</rb><rt>siu-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>阿嗎</rb><rt>ah-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>量量</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收勒</rb><rt>siu-leh</rt><rb>多個</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收勒</rb><rt>siu-leh</rt><rb>缺個</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>夠</rb><rt>keo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>留點落</rb><rt>liu-tin-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>留點</rb><rt>liu-tin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮個</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>蟲</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>臭掉了</rb><rt>tsʽiu-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>曬着</rb><rt>sa-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>煬掉了</rb><rt>yiang-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>收進個</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一倍</rb><rt>ih-be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>阿嗎</rb><rt>ah-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>聖日</rb><rt>sing-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>煮個</rb><rt>ts-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>烹個</rb><rt>pʽang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>烹</rb><rt>pʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>囥東</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>臭</rb><rt>tsʽiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>蟲</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兩日個</rb><rt>liang-nyih-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>安息了</rb><rt>en-sih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>Mô-nô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>芫荽子</rb><rt>nyün-se-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白個</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>扁餅</rb><rt>pin-ping</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>𣥼滿勒</rb><rt>tsi-mun-leh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿嗎</rb><rt>ah-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>囥東</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>裏</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿嗎<sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>ah-mô-go</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>囥東</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>約箱個</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>囥東</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>阿嗎</rb><rt>Ah-mô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法</u><sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>yi-fah-go</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Liang-sing. 就是兩升。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Kʽen* <sup>16</sup> tsih. 看 <sup>16</sup> 節。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Ziu-z* Soh-go tong-si. *Kʽen* <sup>15</sup> tsih. 就是「啥個東西」。看 <sup>15</sup> 節。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[4\] *Ziu-z* Liang-teo. 就是兩斗。（原註）</sup>

### C. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>個</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥幹</rb><rt>soh-ken</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>口渴煞</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-sah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>幾幾乎</rb><rt>kyi-kyi-wu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>河個</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>何烈</rb><rt>ʽO-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪撒</rb><rt>Mô-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利巴</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* S-s. 就是「試試」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Tsang-leng. 就是「爭論」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>山頂頭</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>戶珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂頭</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>贏了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>宕落</rb><rt>dang-loh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>贏了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>喫力</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>戶珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>殺敗</rb><rt>sah-ba</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>載落</rb><rt>tsæ-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記認</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞瑪力</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>塗銷</rb><rt>du-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尼西</rb><rt>Nyi-si</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô*, Læ keh peng shü-li. 或話，來箇本書裏。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Yiæ-ʽo-wô ngô gyi-ʽao. 就是「耶和華我旗號」。（原註）</sup>

---

### C. XVIII.

<ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>MO-SI-GO</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葉羅</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做過個</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葉羅</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革順</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以列撒</rb><rt>Yi-lih-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葉羅</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>擔子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>挑勒動</rb><rt>tʽiao-leh-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安穩穩</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人個</rb><rt>dziang-nying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>才能個</rb><rt>dzæ-neng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>隨時</rb><rt>ze-z</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Jing-ming pông-dzu ngô. 就是「神明幫助我」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 垂頭喪氣。《字語彙解》記 w̆e-w̆e'-ze-ze' 痿痿瘁°瘁°</sup>

### C. XIX.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>三月</rb><rt>sæn-yüih</rt><rb>初一</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>紮營</rb><rt>tsah-ying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田畈裏</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>山面前</rb><rt>sæn-min-zin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>老鷹</rb><rt>lao-ing</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yih-sao</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>列國</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>寶貝</rb><rt>pao-pe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>當面</rb><rt>tông-min</rt><rb>傳撥</rb><rt>djün-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>合口齊動</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>應起來</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>厚厚</rb><rt>ʽeo-ʽeo</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做忌</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箭</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>射</rb><rt>zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>吹勒</rb><rt>cʽü-leh</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>方纔</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>山邊沿</rb><rt>sæn-pin-yin</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>響雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>厚厚</rb><rt>ʽeo-ʽeo</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>響響個</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>營寨裏個</rb><rt>ying-za-li-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>竄上</rb><rt>cʽün-zông</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火爐裏</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>滿山</rb><rt>mun-sæn</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>震動</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>號筒個</rb><rt>ʽAo-dong-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>響勒</rb><rt>hyiang-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>重起來</rb><rt>djong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>頂頭</rb><rt>ting-den</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上去</rb><rt>tseo-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走落去</rb><rt>tseo-loh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>警戒</rb><rt>kying-kyiæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>闖進來</rb><rt>cʽông-tsing-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>看看相</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>山個</rb><rt>Sæn-go</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走落去</rb><rt>tseo-loh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>闖進來</rb><rt>cʽông-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。
