### C. XX.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>泥塑木雕</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>地底下</rb><rt>di-ti-ʽô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嫉邪個</rb><rt>dzih-zia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三四</rb><rt>sæn-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>妄</rb><rt>vông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>妄</rb><rt>vông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>六日</rb><rt>Loh-nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>工</rb><rt>kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>裏向個</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造成</rb><rt>zao-dzing</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向個</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>謊告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驢</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>響雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>號筒個</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>發出個</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退落</rb><rt>tʽe-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>loh-læ</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>威光</rb><rt>w̆e-kwông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>爛泥個</rb><rt>na-nyi-go</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名頭個</rb><rt>ming-deo-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>石頭個</rb><rt>zah-deo-go</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>篤出個</rb><rt>toh-cʽih-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>鑿過個</rb><rt>zoh-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>格步階</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>壇裏</rb><rt>dæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>露出</rb><rt>lu-cʽih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

### C. XXI.

「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>單身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>單身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒囡了</rb><rt>ng-nô-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>單身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>門個</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>敦枋沿</rb><rt>teng-fông-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鑽子</rb><rt>cün-ts</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>一生</rb><rt>ih-sang</rt><rb>一世</rb><rt>ih-si</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia* *fæn* Z-s. 或者翻士師。</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>弗歡喜</rb><rt>feh-hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣穿</rb><rt>i-cʽün</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>情分</rb><rt>dzing-veng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三樣</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>打煞</rb><rt>Tang-sah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>託付</rb><rt>tʽoh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt><rb>謀殺</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭壇裏</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>Tang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿孃個</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>拐勒</rb><rt>Kwa-leh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>賣其掉</rb><rt>ma-gyi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>Tsiu-mô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿孃個</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拳頭</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠勒</rb><rt>kʽao-leh</rt><rb>哀杖</rb><rt>æ-dziang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>聽還</rb><rt>tʽing-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>費事失業個</rb><rt>fi-z-shih-nyih-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>兩日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt><rb>弗死</rb><rt>feh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相打</rb><rt>siang-tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>雙身</rb><rt>shông-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>墮身</rb><rt>do-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>士師</rb><rt>z-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一命</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>一命</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>火燙</rb><rt>ho-tʽông</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>火燙</rb><rt>ho-tʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傷痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>傷痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>抵還</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女個</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>打落</rb><rt>tang-loh</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女個</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>𧤼着</rb><rt>gyüih-djoh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗喫</rb><rt>feh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>牛個</rb><rt>ngeo-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所定個</rb><rt>sô-ding-go</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>例</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>辦法</rb><rt>bæn-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>*z-kʽeh*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Da-iah* jih-ng liang. 大約十五兩。（原註）</sup>

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𢳚開</rb><rt>hyiao-kʽæ</rt><rb>井蓋</rb><rt>tsing-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>蓋好</rb><rt>kæ-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>陷落</rb><rt>ʽæn-loh</rt><rb>井裏</rb><rt>tsing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>牲口個</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XXII.

「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ZIAH</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>五頭</rb><rt>ng-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四隻</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>碰見</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>盜賊</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt><rb>挖</rb><rt>w̆eh</rt><rb>牆洞</rb><rt>ziang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>填命</rb><rt>din-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>填命</rb><rt>din-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>賊贓</rb><rt>zah-tsông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>賠還個</rb><rt>be-wæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>偷個</rb><rt>tʽeo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>喫掉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄園裏個</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li-go</rt><rb>頂好個</rb><rt>ting-hao-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>飛散</rb><rt>fi-sæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>刺棚裏</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>延開</rb><rt>yin-kʽæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>對間個</rb><rt>te-kæn-go</rt><rb>麥裏</rb><rt>mah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>田稻</rb><rt>din-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>起火個</rb><rt>kyʽi-ho-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-Wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>代管</rb><rt>dæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>賊</rb><rt>zeh</rt><rb>一抲着</rb><rt>ih-kʽô-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>抲弗着</rb><rt>kʽô-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>審問</rb><rt>sing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>疏失個</rb><rt>su-shih-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定罪個</rb><rt>ding-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *fæn* Z-s. 或翻士師。</sup>

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>代管</rb><rt>dæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>踏煞</rb><rt>dah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>踏煞個</rb><rt>dah-sah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>借來個</rb><rt>tsia-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>賠</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>賠還</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>貰來個</rb><rt>shü-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>水田</rb><rt>shü-din</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>許配個</rb><rt>hyü-pʽe-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt><rb>同房</rb><rt>dong-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>聘定</rb><rt>pʽing-ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>決定</rb><rt>kyüih-ding</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>稱</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聘定</rb><rt>pʽing-ding</rt><rb>大姑娘個</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>邪術個</rb><rt>zia-jih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆生</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>同牀個</rb><rt>dong-zông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>專門</rb><rt>cün-meng</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>出門人</rb><rt>Cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吵擾</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>孤兒</rb><rt>Kwu-r</rt><rb>寡婦</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>生火</rb><rt>sang-ho</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>寡婦</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>孤兒</rb><rt>kwu-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>窮</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>貪利個</rb><rt>tʽen-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>利鈿</rb><rt>li-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鄰舍家個</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>當頭</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山個</rb><rt>loh-sæn-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身底個</rb><rt>sing-ti-go</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>皮膚個</rb><rt>bi-fu-go</rt><rb>穿着</rb><rt>cʽün-tsiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>慈悲個</rb><rt>dz-pe-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官長</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘗新個</rb><rt>zông-sing-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>獻上來</rb><rt>hyin-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>遲緩</rb><rt>dzi-wun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頭一胎個</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聖潔個</rb><rt>sing-kyih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>咬傷個</rb><rt>ngao-sông-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>狗</rb><rt>keo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

---

### C. XXIII.

「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>謠言</rb><rt>yiao-yin</rt><rb>傳開去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>絞手</rb><rt>gao-siu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>大衆</rb><rt>da-cong</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>徇</rb><rt>shing</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>情面</rb><rt>dzing-min</rt><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>包庇</rb><rt>pao-pi</rt><rb>窮人個</rb><rt>gyüong-nying-go</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牽轉來</rb><rt>kyʽin-cün-læ</rt><rb>歸還</rb><rt>kwe-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>揹弗動</rb><rt>pe-feh-dong</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>弗情願</rb><rt>feh-dzing-nyün</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必巧勒</rb><rt>pih-kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>離遠</rb><rt>li-yün</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>嘸罪個</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>賄賂</rb><rt>hwe-lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>賄賂</rb><rt>hwe-lu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>瞎</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>彎曲</rb><rt>w̆æn-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吵擾</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>六</rb><rt>Loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歇落</rb><rt>hyih-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空過</rb><rt>kʽong-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>窮</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>剩落個</rb><rt>dzing-loh-go</rt><rb>多頭</rb><rt>to-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>六日</rb><rt>Loh-nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>使女個</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>張揚</rb><rt>tsiang-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>當心</rb><rt>tông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>出口</rb><rt>cʽih-kʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>每年</rb><rt>Me-nyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>Vu-kao-tsih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞筆</rb><rt>Üô-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嘸酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>收割個</rb><rt>Siu-keh-go</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>種田個</rb><rt>cong-din-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>上倉個</rb><rt>Zông-tsʽông-go</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>低下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>收進個</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>每年</rb><rt>Me-nyin</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭物個</rb><rt>tsi-veh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水個</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭物個</rb><rt>tsi-veh-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>結個</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>殿裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃個</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>奶裏</rb><rt>na-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>S-tsʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>保護</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>備辦個</rb><rt>be-bæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>謹慎</rb><rt>kying-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>過失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>對頭個</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>S-tsʽa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份個</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>榜樣</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆毀</rb><rt>tsʽah-hwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>病症</rb><rt>bing-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>墮身個</rb><rt>do-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>弗生個</rb><rt>feh-sang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日子個</rb><rt>nyih-ts-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>威嚴</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>pe</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>黃蜂</rb><rt>wông-fong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕走</rb><rt>ken-tseo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-lah</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>荒掉</rb><rt>hwông-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>茂盛</rb><rt>meo-zing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大河</rb><rt>do-ʽo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立約</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>陷</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>圈套裏</rb><rt>kyʽün-tʽao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Pah-lah ʽÔ. 就是<u>伯拉</u>河。（原註）</sup>

### C. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比戶</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>七十個</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上來</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>合口齊動</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所話個</rb><rt>sô-wô-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-loh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>早天亮</rb><rt>tsao-tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>謝恩</rb><rt>zia-eng</rt><rb>祭個</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>𣥼來</rb><rt>tsi-læ</rt><rb>盤</rb><rt>bun</rt><rb>裏</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>扎來</rb><rt>tsah-læ</rt><rb>壇裏</rb><rt>dæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>立約個</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所話個</rb><rt>sô-wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>扎來</rb><rt>tsah-læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立約個</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比戶</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>七十個</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>走上去</rb><rt>tseo-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>青青個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>鋪的</rb><rt>pʽu-tih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>體質</rb><rt>tʽi-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>梗青</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>石頭個</rb><rt>zah-deo-go</rt><rb>碑牌</rb><rt>pe-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫個</rb><rt>sia-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>辦事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>戶珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住了</rb><rt>tsô-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>雲個</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山頂頭</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XXV.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>甘心</rb><rt>ken-sing</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>染紅個</rb><rt>nyin-ʽong-go</rt><rb>雄羊皮</rb><rt>yüong-yiang-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海狗個</rb><rt>hæ-keo-go</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>點亮個</rb><rt>tin-liang-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>傅個</rb><rt>fu-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香氣個</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嵌來</rb><rt>kʽæn-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>胸牌裏個</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba-li-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>起造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>聖所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看個</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>器皿個</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Li-i. 就是裏衣。（原註）</sup>

<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>織機</rb><rt>tsih-kyi</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>織出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基路冰</rb><rt>kyi-lu-ping</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>花紋個</rb><rt>hwô-veng-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>掛來</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>四根</rb><rt>s-keng</rt><rb>包金</rb><rt>pao-kying</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>金鈎</rb><rt>kying-keo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>掛</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>鈎子下</rb><rt>keo-ts-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>清清</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隔開</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt><rb>聖所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>至聖所</rb><rt>Ts-sing-sô</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>至聖所</rb><rt>Ts-sing-sô</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>恩座</rb><rt>eng-zo</rt><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>案桌</rb><rt>En-coh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>擺來</rb><rt>pa-læ</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>案桌</rb><rt>en-coh</rt><rb>相對</rb><rt>siang-te</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>案桌</rb><rt>en-coh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>擺</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>繡花</rb><rt>siu-hwô</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做出個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-go</rt><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>門簾</rb><rt>meng-lin</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>門簾</rb><rt>meng-lin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>五根</rb><rt>ng-keng</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>包金</rb><rt>pao-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鈎子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鑄</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。」
