### C. XXVII.

「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>IAO</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>Tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四邊</rb><rt>s-pin</rt><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>四邊</rb><rt>s-pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>四隻</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>做塊</rb><rt>tso-kwʽe</rt><rb>料作</rb><rt>liao-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>包出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>𣥼灰個</rb><rt>tsi-hwe-go</rt><rb>盤</rb><rt>bun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火杴</rb><rt>ho-hyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>碗</rb><rt>un</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>肉鉤</rb><rt>nyüoh-keo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>火爐</rb><rt>ho-lu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>銅絲個</rb><rt>dong-s-go</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>網個</rb><rt>mông-go</rt><rb>四角</rb><rt>s-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>花邊</rb><rt>hwô-pin</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>半橛裏</rb><rt>pun-gyüih-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>槓</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>包出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>Yü-ziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>朝南個</rb><rt>dziao-nen-go</rt><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一邊</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>廿根</rb><rt>nyiæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>廿個</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>柱子個</rb><rt>djü-ts-go</rt><rb>鈎子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>橫檔</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>廿根</rb><rt>nyiæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>廿個</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>柱子個</rb><rt>djü-ts-go</rt><rb>鈎子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>橫檔</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt><rb>闊個</rb><rt>kwʽeh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>十根</rb><rt>jih-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>蕩邊個</rb><rt>Dông-pin-go</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>ah-ma</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>三根</rb><rt>sæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圍牆個</rb><rt>yü-ziang-go</rt><rb>門簾</rb><rt>meng-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>廿</rb><rt>nyiæn</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>繡花</rb><rt>siu-hwô</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>四根</rb><rt>s-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>Yü-ziang</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>橫檔</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鈎子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>Yü-ziang</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銅個</rb><rt>dong-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong-li</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>釘子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>圍牆個</rb><rt>yü-ziang-go</rt><rb>釘子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Kʽen* C. 26. 2. 看 C. 26. 2.（原註）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>清潔個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>舂</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>燈盞</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>時刻</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>整頓</rb><rt>tsing-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」

---

### C. XXVIII.

「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比戶</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大瑪</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>豔麗</rb><rt>yin-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>智慧</rb><rt>cü-we</rt><rb>靈性個</rb><rt>ling-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>套衣</rb><rt>Tʽao-i</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>花衣</rb><rt>Hwô-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>帽</rb><rt>Mao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>Ta</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>織</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>花頭</rb><rt>hwô-deo</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>織出個</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>披肩</rb><rt>pʽi-kyin</rt><rb>連來</rb><rt>lin-læ</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt><rb>連攏</rb><rt>lin-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>織出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>花紋個</rb><rt>hwô-veng-go</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以弗</u>個</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兩塊</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉裏</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉裏</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>次序</rb><rt>tsʽ-jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>刻</rb><rt>Kʽeh</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>刻印</rb><rt>kʽeh-ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩塊</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉裏</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>嵌</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子裏</rb><rt>zo-ts-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩塊</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以弗</u>個</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>記認個</rb><rt>kyi-nying-go</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記認</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>編</rb><rt>Pin</rt><rb>兩根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>絲</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纏攏</rb><rt>dzin-long</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>繩</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>勾住</rb><rt>keo-djü</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>織出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>審判個</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尺工</rb><rt>tsʽih-kong</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以弗</u>個</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>藍</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>轉方個</rb><rt>cün-fông-go</rt><rb>兩層</rb><rt>liang-zeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一庹</rb><rt>ih-tʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>一庹</rb><rt>ih-tʽoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>嵌</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四埭</rb><rt>s-da</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>頭一</rb><rt>deo-ih</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瑪瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>淡黃</rb><rt>dæn-wông</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>透光</rb><rt>tʽeo-kwông</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蔥玉</rb><rt>tsʽong-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>青玉</rb><rt>tsʽing-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>金剛石</rb><rt>kying-kông-zah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>Di-sæn</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赤玉</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>瑪瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫玉</rb><rt>ts-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>第四</rb><rt>Di-s</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>綠玉</rb><rt>loh-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蒼玉</rb><rt>tsʽông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嵌來</rb><rt>kʽæn-læ</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子裏</rb><rt>zo-ts-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>Nyüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>塊</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>胸牌個</rb><rt>hyüong-ba-go</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兩根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>絲</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>纏攏</rb><rt>dzin-long</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>繩</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>hyüong-ba</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>胸牌個</rb><rt>hyüong-ba-go</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒香個</rb><rt>siao-hyiang-go</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>Dziang</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四邊</rb><rt>s-pin</rt><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>nyi</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做塊</rb><rt>tso-kwʽe</rt><rb>料作個</rb><rt>liao-tsoh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>包來</rb><rt>pao-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>四角</rb><rt>s-koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>花邊</rb><rt>hwô-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皂莢木</rb><rt>zao-kyiah-moh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>槓</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>包勒</rb><rt>pao-leh</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>壇</rb><rt>Dæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>擺</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>上頭個</rb><rt>zông-deo-go</rt><rb>恩座</rb><rt>eng-zo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>燒香</rb><rt>siao-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>周作</rb><rt>tsiu-tsoh</rt><rb>燈盞</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>燒香</rb><rt>siao-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>燈盞</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>燒香</rb><rt>siao-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>燒香</rb><rt>siao-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每年</rb><rt>me-nyin</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>贖罪祭個</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>角裏</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>每年</rb><rt>me-nyin</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>挨戶冊</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>23</sup> </sup>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>傅個</rb><rt>fu-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>香料個</rb><rt>hyiang-liao-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>傅個</rb><rt>fu-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>搽來</rb><rt>djô-læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>法箱裏</rb><rt>fah-siang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>案桌</rb><rt>en-coh</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>香壇</rb><rt>hyiang-dæn</rt></ruby>；<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>燔祭個</rb><rt>væn-tsi-go</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>浴盆</rb><rt>yüoh-beng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>傅來</rb><rt>fu-læ</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拒絕</rb><rt>gyü-djih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>拿達</rb><rt>nô-dah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>士以勒</rb><rt>z-yi-leh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>赫</u>比拿</rb><rt>heh-pi-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>淨個</rb><rt>zing-go</rt><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt><rb>和帶攏</rb><rt>ʽo-tæn-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>分量</rb><rt>veng-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>和香個</rb><rt>ʽo-hyiang-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加勒</rb><rt>kô-leh</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和帶攏</rb><rt>ʽo-tæn-long</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>舂勒</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>細細</rb><rt>si-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>約箱個</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>Pih-iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拒絕</rb><rt>gyü-djih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」
